Annual Report 2025-26
Marico Bangladesh Limited
S C A L I N G G R O W T H S U S TA I N A B LY


--- PDF page 2 ---
Marico Bangladesh Limited has fostered a growth journey of 26 years winning the 
hearts and minds of millions of households in Bangladesh. It is now one of the leading 
corporate entities in Fast Moving Consumer Goods Industry specializing in Personal 
Care Space. Since its listing in 2009, the Company has maintained its position as a 
distinguished blue-chip stock on both the Dhaka and Chittagong Stock Exchanges. 
Guided by its commitment to creating sustainable value, Marico Bangladesh continues 
to make a meaningful difference in the lives of all stakeholders. With a diverse portfolio 
of trusted brands, the Company today reaches 4 out of every 5 Bangladeshi Households, 
underscoring its scale, relevance, and enduring consumer trust. 
Welcome to 
Bangladesh Limited
marico

Marico Bangladesh Limited delivered strong, consistent, and resilient growth in FY 2025–26, despite navigating 
multiple macroeconomic challenges during the year. This performance was driven by disciplined strategic 
planning, operational excellence, a portfolio of trusted brands, and a steadfast consumer-centric approach.
Looking ahead, the Company remains committed to sustaining this growth momentum. It will continue to 
embed responsible business practices and environmental stewardship into its core strategy, with key initiatives 
focused on resource efficiency, climate action, responsible sourcing, and reducing environmental impact while 
enhancing resilience.
In parallel, the Company is investing in product development, digital capabilities, and operational excellence to 
remain competitive in a rapidly evolving marketplace. These investments are closely aligned with its sustainability 
agenda, enabling Marico Bangladesh to meet current consumer needs and support future generations.
SCALING GROWTH SUSTAINABLY

 GROWING TOGETHER 
Marico Bangladesh Limited is driven by its purpose of 
creating a meaningful difference in the lives of all its 
stakeholders. Anchored in this belief, the year under review 
reflects the resilience and dedication of our members. 
Their unwavering commitment has been instrumental 
in achieving benchmark performance, advancing our 
social responsibility initiatives, and ensuring operational 
excellence despite facing multiple challenges - truly 
embodying the spirit of “Be the Impact!”
Guided by our strategic priorities and core values, 
Mariconians successfully navigated another year marked 
by an uncertain and volatile external environment. Despite 
these challenges, we delivered growth and profitability, 
safeguarded the health and safety of our members and 
extended workforce, scaled up our social initiatives, 
and strengthened our portfolio of leading brands. While 
Financial Year 2025-26 had its own unique challenges, it 
has ultimately made us stronger, more agile, and better 
equipped to respond to evolving consumer needs and a 
dynamic business landscape. We have continued to create 
value through our quality products, robust value chain, 
strong talent proposition, sound governance practices, 
and responsible corporate citizenship.
This year further reinforced our commitment to community 
engagement, 
sustainable 
business 
practices, 
and 
adherence to social compliance standards. We take pride 
in our collective achievements and remain committed to 
demonstrating that a purpose-driven and impact-focused 
approach can deliver sustainable performance, both now 
and in the future.
Together, we remain dedicated to building a brighter and 
more sustainable future, creating a lasting and positive 
legacy for generations to come.

WELCOME NOTE 
FROM THE CHAIRMAN
Saugata Gupta 
Chairman
This report is more than 
a record of financial 
performance. It is a reflection 
of the trust you have placed 
on us, the resilience we have 
built together, and the shared 
commitment that continues 
to define our progress.
It is my privilege to welcome you to Marico Bangladesh's 
Annual Report for the Financial Year ended 31 March 2026 
(FY 2025-26), a document that reflects not only the year's 
performance but the enduring values, strategic clarity, and 
collective effort that continue to shape our progress.
This report is more than a record of financial performance. It 
is a reflection of the trust you have placed on us, the resilience 
we have built together, and the shared commitment that 
continues to define our progress. In a year marked by 
economic headwinds, persistent inflationary pressures, and 
global uncertainty, Marico Bangladesh delivered another 
year of meaningful growth - a testament to the strength of 
our business fundamentals, the enduring relevance of our 
brands, and the dedication of our people.
What fills me with particular pride is not only what we 
achieved, but how we achieved it. We remained guided by 
our values, committed to responsible business practices, 
and resolute in our focus on creating sustainable value for 
all stakeholders. We strengthened our market position while 
continuing to invest in innovation, operational excellence, and 
the long-term capabilities that will underpin future growth.
None of this is possible without the people and partners 
who stand beside us. I extend my sincere gratitude to our 
consumers for their enduring trust in our brands; to our 
business partners for their collaboration and commitment; 
to our members, whose passion and hard work continue 
to be the foundation of everything we accomplish; and to 
you, our shareholders, for the confidence and support you 
consistently extend to Marico Bangladesh.
As we look ahead, we do so with genuine confidence. 
Periods of change, in our experience, create the greatest 
opportunities for those who are prepared to adapt and 
lead. With a portfolio of trusted brands, a culture rooted in 
innovation, and a talented team dedicated to excellence, 
Marico Bangladesh is well positioned to navigate what lies 
ahead and to create enduring value for generations to come.
Thank you for being part of our journey.

ABOUT THE REPORT 
Report Objectives 
At Marico Bangladesh, we prioritize keeping our shareholders and stakeholders fully informed about our 
business operations. With our 2025-26 Annual Report, our commitment to transparency shines through as 
we provide clear and relevant information that offers insights into the health and strength of our operations. 
Our goal is to empower our shareholders to make informed assessments of Marico Bangladesh’s value 
creation activities, business performance, and future prospects. By delivering comprehensive and transparent 
reporting, we aim to foster trust and confidence in our organization and its endeavors.
Reporting Period
This report serves as a valuable resource, offering readers material information and insightful analysis of 
our performance for the fiscal year spanning from 1st April 2025 to 31st March 2026 (FY 2025-26). When 
considering external factors, risks, and opportunities, we adopt a long-term perspective. This approach 
equips readers with the necessary tools to effectively evaluate Marico’s capabilities in value creation, 
sustainability, and future prospects.
The investor relations section on the website contains the annual reports, supplemented by additional 
information on governance and shareholding. Please visit: https://marico.com/bangladesh/investors
The Scope and Boundary of the Report 
This report adheres to the Integrated Reporting framework established by the International Integrated 
Reporting Council (IIRC). Our aim is to offer a transparent and holistic overview of our business, encompassing 
its culture and financial performance. Within this framework, we have included all aspects of our business 
operations, both internal and external impacts, to identify trends, opportunities, and risks that could influence 
our ability to create value over time. Furthermore, this report delves into various dimensions of our company, 
including our purpose, strategic intent, business performance, social responsibility, and sustainability 
commitments. By addressing stakeholder interests and highlighting key risks facing the business, we strive 
to provide a comprehensive and insightful portrayal of Marico Bangladesh's operations and aspirations.
Materiality 
This report is dedicated to disclosing vital information concerning matters that have a substantial impact on 
our ability to create value over the short, medium, and long term. The key material matters we address in this 
report are as follows.
Business model resilience 
in a rapidly changing 
consumer, business and 
macro environment 
Good governance 
and regulatory 
complexity 
Role of Marico in 
social responsibility 
and sustainability

Assurance 
The Board has diligently applied its collective expertise to compile and present Marico Bangladesh’s 
Report, recognizing its responsibility to uphold the integrity of this document through the implementation 
of sound governance practices and robust internal reporting procedures. The financial disclosures and 
reports contained herein received the approval of the Board on 29th April 2026.
Definition of Terms 
Unless otherwise indicated or defined, the following terms, wherever appearing in this report shall have the 
following meanings: 
•	
“Act” means the Companies Act, 1994 
•	
“Board” means the Board of Directors of Marico Bangladesh Limited 
•	
“BSEC” means the Bangladesh Securities & Exchange Commission 
•	
“Code” means the Corporate Governance Code issued by the Bangladesh Securities & Exchange Commission 
under Notification No. SEC/CMRRCD/2006-158/207/Admin/80, Dated: 3 June 2018 
•	
“CoC” means Marico’s Unified Code of Conduct
•	
“Listing Regulations” means the Dhaka Stock Exchange (Listing) Regulations 2015 and Chittagong Stock 
Exchange (Listing) Regulations 2015
Feedback and Comments 
We welcome your feedback on this annual report. Please email us your views at 
secretarial.mbl@marico.com  
Forward-looking Statements 
In this Annual Report, forward-looking statements are present, characterized by terms like ‘might’, 
‘forecast’, ‘anticipate’, ‘project’, ‘may’, ‘believe’, ‘predict’, ‘expect’, ‘continue’, ‘will’, ‘estimate’, ‘target’, and similar 
expressions, concerning Marico Bangladesh Limited's financial conditions, results, operations, and business. 
These projections and forecasts inherently entail risk and uncertainty, as they relate to future events or 
outcomes, such as anticipated improvements in business performance or potential decisions to be made. 
It's crucial to understand that these forward-looking statements do not assure future operating, financial, 
or other results due to the unpredictable nature of future risks and uncertainties. Therefore, readers should 
note that this Annual Report for 2025-26 does not serve as a profit forecast, nor do the statements contained 
herein provide any guarantee that the potential outcomes mentioned in these forward-looking statements 
will be achieved.

WE ARE  
MARICO 
As one of the fastest growing consumer goods companies in 
Bangladesh, we are driven by our purpose of Making A Difference 
in the lives of all those we touch. Over the last one decade, Marico 
Bangladesh has emerged as one of the top three FMCG companies in 
the country, earning recognition for its consistent growth driven by a 
well-diversified Personal Care and Foods portfolio spanning Haircare, 
Skincare, Baby Care, Male Grooming, and Foods. The Company’s 
flagship brand, Parachute Advansed, continues to be a trusted 
household name and holds a leading position in the Hair Oil category. 
Since its inception in 1999, Marico Bangladesh has operated as a 
subsidiary of Marico Limited, a multinational FMCG powerhouse 
selling its products in 50+ countries. Upholding its commitment 
to “Made in Bangladesh,” 99.9% of the Company’s portfolio is 
manufactured locally, reinforcing its role in strengthening domestic 
capabilities. Beyond serving the local market, Marico Bangladesh 
also exports to India, Sri Lanka, Nepal, the Middle East, Egypt, and 
Vietnam, showcasing the global competitiveness of its brands.

With our ‘One Marico’ spirit and ‘People First’ 
approach
We’re witnessing rapidly changing times, new 
consumer preferences, new ways of work and new 
talent aspirations. As an organization focused on 
rapid growth and creating shared value, carving and 
retaining our position as an ‘employer of choice’ 
means attracting, retaining and developing talent in 
line with the changing talent aspirations.  
Go Beyond, Grow Beyond, Be The Impact - our 
TVP centres around nurturing the growth of our 
members, by fostering a diverse, empowering 
and transparent work environment. This defining 
statement encompasses what Marico stands for, 
what it offers, and what it admires. 
Our TVP outlines the ambition for the Marico of 
tomorrow as well as the desire to make a difference 
by creating a lasting impact.
Go Beyond: This pillar believes in encouraging 
members to take calculated risks and aims to 
motivate members to look beyond the bend by 
unleashing their curiosity and inculcating a 
restless drive to grow and disrupt. 
Grow Beyond: Grow Beyond as a pillar strives 
to establish a trust-based and transparent 
work 
environment 
where 
everybody 
is inspired to embrace diversity and 
differences. It 
aims 
to 
encourage 
collaboration by breaking silos to unlock 
opportunities for inclusive growth. 
Be the Impact: This pillar encourages 
members to take actions that are large 
enough to make an impact and close 
enough to care, thereby empowering 
them to drive positive change in the 
greater society. The company will aid the 
individual growth of members and help 
cultivate their passion and purpose to “Make 
a Difference” by enabling them to touch the 
lives of people every day.
GO BEYOND, GROW BEYOND, 
BE THE IMPACT

 OUR CORPORATE VALUE 
Our Statement of Purpose
Making a difference in the lives of all those we touch
Nurturing and enabling individuals to reach their fullest potential is paramount. The essence of business lies not solely in 
profits but in its purpose. At Marico, we are committed to defining, creating, and delivering value to all stakeholders—be it 
shareholders, consumers, team members, or business partners. Our journey of sustainable growth is rooted in a culture that 
fosters empowerment and encourages ownership among our team members. As a multi-stakeholder organization dedicated 
to fostering shared value, our purpose is ingrained in our culture, guiding our business practices and demonstrating our duty, 
responsibility, and dedication to enhancing the well-being of all those associated with us.

MARICO’S SCRIPT PERFORMANCE
Throughout the financial year 2025-26, Marico Bangladesh 
Limited (MBL) witnessed a sustained positive momentum 
in its share performance. The stability of MBL's share price 
over the year is a testament to the market's confidence 
in our strong brand portfolio, our consistent and growing 
performance within the consumer goods industry, and our 
unwavering commitment to robust governance practices.
During the financial year 2025–26, MBL maintained 
its commitment to delivering consistent returns to its 
shareholders. The Company declared and disbursed 
three interim dividends of total 1575% on the face value 
of equity shares. Furthermore, the Board of Directors has 
recommended a final cash dividend of 500%, subject to 
approval at the upcoming 26th Annual General Meeting, 
taking the total dividend payout for the year to 2075%. This 
competitive payout reflects not only our solid financial 
health but also our commitment to creating long-term 
value for our shareholders. 
Despite the broader market volatility, driven by high 
inflation and economic instability, MBL’s share price 
demonstrated 
remarkable 
stability. 
This 
resilience 
highlights the intrinsic strength of our business model, the 
effectiveness of our strategic initiatives, and the market’s 
recognition of our sustained operational excellence. The 
stability of our share price underscores the robustness 
of our financial strategies and the soundness of our 
corporate governance. The positive trajectory of our share 
price amid economic challenges is indicative of our ability 
to navigate and mitigate macroeconomic risks effectively. 
Our proactive measures in managing inflationary pressures 
and maintaining supply chain efficiency have been crucial 
in sustaining investor confidence.
Moreover, 
the 
dividend 
payout 
and 
stable 
share 
performance are reflective of our strategic investments 
in capacity expansion, innovation, and new product 
development. By consistently reinvesting in our operations 
and prioritizing sustainable growth, we have managed 
to enhance our competitive position in the market. The 
market’s favorable response to our stock is a clear indication 
of the confidence investors have in our long- term growth 
prospects and our ability to deliver consistent returns.
In conclusion, the financial year 2025-26 has been a year 
of robust performance and strategic growth for Marico 
Bangladesh. As we continue to navigate the economic 
landscape, our focus remains on driving sustainable 
growth, maximizing shareholder value, and maintaining the 
trust and confidence of our investors. The stability of our 
share price amidst economic turbulence are a testament 
to our resilience and the strong foundations upon which 
Marico Bangladesh is built.

OTHER SHAREHOLDER DISCLOSURES 
Investor Relations
Marico Bangladesh Limited maintains an effective 
investor relations process rooted in the philosophy of 
providing transparent, timely information and responses 
to our valued shareholders. Our commitment to open 
communication 
is 
demonstrated 
through 
regular 
interactions with the investment community via periodic 
calls and individual meetings. Additionally, we ensure that 
all necessary information about the Company is readily 
available to investors by regularly updating the 'Investor 
Relations' section of our website.
To streamline dividend disbursement and efficiently 
manage dividend-related queries from shareholders, the 
Company has established a comprehensive Dividend 
Distribution Policy. This policy ensures that dividends are 
distributed smoothly and that shareholder inquiries are 
handled promptly and effectively.
For regular updates and to address any queries regarding 
the latest developments in the Company’s business 
and shares, shareholders and the investor community 
are encouraged to reach out. They can send an email 
to secretarial.mbl@marico.com or call our corporate 
office at +88 (02) 222297139. This proactive approach 
underscores our dedication to maintaining robust and 
transparent communication with our investors, reinforcing 
their trust and confidence in Marico Bangladesh Limited.
Redressal of Investor Complaint
Our Investor Relations team is dedicated to prioritizing 
and addressing investor queries and complaints with the 
utmost efficiency and transparency. Ensuring timely and 
satisfactory resolution is key to maintaining the trust of our 
valued shareholders. General queries of shareholders are 
related to receipt of dividends. To manage these concerns 
effectively, we have established a comprehensive 
Redressal Mechanism:
Prompt Acknowledgment
Upon receiving a query or 
complaint, we immediately 
acknowledge it and provide an 
estimated timeline for resolution 
to reassure the shareholder that 
their concern is being addressed.
Detailed Review and Analysis
Our Investor Relations team 
carefully reviews the nature 
of the query or complaint, 
conducting a thorough analysis 
to identify the required steps for 
resolution.
In-depth Investigation
If necessary, a detailed 
investigation is undertaken 
in collaboration with relevant 
departments to collect all 
pertinent information, ensuring a 
well-informed response.


--- PDF page 40 ---
39
Annual Report 2025-26

 Corporate

Auditor's Report &

Shareholders' 
Information
Efficient Resolution
Based on the findings, appropriate actions are taken 
to resolve the issue. This may involve reissuing 
dividends, updating shareholder information, or 
providing additional clarifications as needed.
Consistent Communication
Throughout the process, we maintain open and 
consistent communication with the shareholder, 
providing regular updates on the status of their query 
or complaint to keep them informed and reassured.
Soliciting Feedback
After the issue is resolved, we seek feedback from 
the shareholder to ensure their satisfaction and to 
identify any areas for potential improvement in our 
processes.
Thorough Documentation and Reporting
All queries and complaints are meticulously 
documented, and comprehensive reports are 
generated for periodic review. This practice helps 
us continuously enhance our Investor Relations 
protocols and address any recurring issues  
more effectively.
When a complaint is received, the Investor Relations team 
promptly acknowledges it and verifies the identity of the 
shareholder by confirming:
•	
Shareholder BO ID
•	
Shareholder Name
•	
Shareholder Bank Details
After verifying these details, the Investor Relations team 
addresses their queries and provides the necessary 
information. Additionally, investors can register their 
complaints and queries through a written application 
addressed to the Company Secretary.
Stakeholder Redress Channels
Email
secretarial.mbl@marico.com
Contact Number
+88 (02) 222297139
Address
The Glass House, Level-06, Plot- 
02, Block-SE (B), Gulshan Avenue, 
Dhaka-1212
Lodging a Complaint
A complaint can be lodged by the investor through the 
channels described above or via the designated email 
secretarial.mbl@marico.com
Acknowledgement
The complaint is forwarded to the complaint officer/
necessary department to resolve the said complaint in a 
time-bound and satisfactory manner.
Investigation
A thorough investigation process is launched based on 
impartiality and fairness regarding the specific details of 
the complaint. This process usually involves contacting 
the parties concerned, examining relevant documents, 
and conducting other necessary assessments.
Communication
A communication channel is maintained to keep investors 
informed of the progress of their complaint, through 
which we demonstrate our seriousness in addressing the 
complaint.
Closure
The complaint is considered closed when a satisfactory 
resolution is arrived at and acceptable to the investor 
concerned.

Career Highlights
Saugata Gupta, the distinguished Chairman of Marico 
Bangladesh Limited since 2013, also serves as the Managing 
Director & Chief Executive Officer of Marico Limited. He joined 
Marico in 2004 as the Head of Marketing and was elevated 
to CEO of India Business in 2007. In 2014, he took over as 
Managing Director of the Marico Limited.
A dynamic leader with more than 35 years of experience, Mr. 
Gupta is renowned for his transformative leadership, driving 
Marico to significant growth and expanding its global presence. 
He has helped transform Marico into a high performing 
business with consistent, profitable and sustainable growth 
and best-in-class governance. His leadership has fostered a 
culture of transparency, integrity and accountability, ensuring 
Marico's compliance with international standards and 
strengthening investor confidence. During his tenure, Marico 
has scaled up its presence to over 25 countries spread across 
emerging markets of Asia, Africa and Middle East.
Over the years, Marico has been recognised across various 
forums for its commitment to sustainability and accountability 
in business operations. Marico has been featured in the 
‘LEADERSHIP’ category on the IFC-BSE-IiAS Indian Corporate 
Governance Scorecard as assessed by IiAS for 6 consecutive 
years. Marico was awarded the Economic Times Corporate 
Citizen of the year 2022 in recognition of its unwavering 
commitment towards adopting highest ESG standards and 
Marico is among the few Indian companies to be rated “AA” in 
MSCI’s ESG Ratings.

Career Highlights
Mr. Zakir Ahmed Khan inducted to the Board of Marico 
Bangladesh Limited on September 22, 2020. He is a 
distinguished bureaucrat renowned for his exemplary 
service as the former Finance Secretary to the Government 
of Bangladesh. With an academic foundation rooted in 
Economics, Mr. Khan obtained his MA from the University of 
Dhaka and pursued further studies, earning an MBA from Vrije 
University, Brussels, Belgium. His quest for knowledge led him 
to delve into Development Economics and Administration at 
the Colorado State University as a Hubert Humphrey Fellow. 
Before embarking on his illustrious bureaucratic journey, Mr. 
Khan briefly served as a Research Associate at the Bureau 
of Economic Research and as a Lecturer in Economics at 
the University of Dhaka. His academic prowess extended to 
the Department of Finance at the same institution, where he 
served as a part-time Lecturer. 
Throughout his distinguished 40 year career in public service, 
Mr. Khan held pivotal roles within the Bangladesh Audit and 
Accounts Department, as well as in key Ministries such as 
Establishment, Finance, Commerce, and Cabinet Division. 
Notably, he served as Finance Secretary and Secretary of 
the Internal Resources Division, as well as Chairman of the 
National Board of Revenue for approximately five years.
Mr. Khan's expertise transcended national boundaries, as 
evidenced by his role as a senior national consultant in the 
public sector financial reform program of the Government 
of Bangladesh. He also served as an External Auditor to 
esteemed international organizations such as the United 
Nations, United Nations Development Programme, United 
Nations Fund for Population Activities, United Nations 
Industrial Development Organization, and United Nations 
Economic Commission for Latin America. 
Prior to his retirement in early 2009, Mr. Khan contributed 
significantly to global governance as the Alternate Executive 
Director representing Bangladesh, Bhutan, India, and Sri 
Lanka at the World Bank. In addition to his illustrious career in 
public service, Mr. Khan continues to contribute his expertise 
as Chairman of Palli Karma-Sahayak Foundation (PKSF) and 
Pioneer Insurance Limited. He also serves as Director of the 
Policy Research Institute (PRI).
Directorship in Other Entities: 
•	 Pioneer Insurance Limited 
•	 Policy Research Institute (PRI)

Career Highlights
Mr. Masud Khan was inducted into the Board of Marico 
Bangladesh Limited for his second stint as an Independent 
Director, effective October 27, 2025. He is a distinguished 
corporate leader with over 46 years of extensive experience 
across leading multinational and local conglomerates in 
Bangladesh. He currently serves as the Chairman of Unilever 
Consumer Care Ltd. (formerly GlaxoSmithKline Bangladesh) 
and as Chief Advisor to the Board of Crown Cement Group.
Mr. Khan's professional journey includes a remarkable 18-
year tenure as the Chief Financial Officer of LafargeHolcim 
Bangladesh, where he played a pivotal role in the formation of 
the company and in shaping its financial and strategic direction. 
Prior to that, he served for two decades in progressively senior 
finance and leadership roles at British American Tobacco, 
both in Bangladesh and internationally. In both organizations, 
Mr. Khan delivered outstanding results and left a legacy of 
impactful achievements in financial stewardship, corporate 
governance, and business transformation.
He holds several key governance positions, currently serving 
as an Independent Director and Chairman of the Audit 
Committee of British American Tobacco Bangladesh. He has 
previously served in similar capacities at Marico Bangladesh 
and Berger Paints Bangladesh.
An active contributor to the public discourse on economic, 
business, and professional issues, Mr. Khan is a regular 
columnist in national newspapers and industry publications. 
He frequently appears on electronic media and is a sought-
after speaker at academic institutions and professional 
forums including the Institute of Chartered Accountants of 
Bangladesh (ICAB) and ACCA. 
Notably, he has been the top-ranked guest lecturer at the 
Institute of Chartered Accountants of Bangladesh (ICAB) for 
the past 46 years—a passion he continues to pursue despite 
his demanding professional commitments, driven by his 
deep belief in mentoring and shaping the next generation of 
business and finance leaders.
Mr. Khan earned his Bachelor of Commerce (Honours) degree 
from St. Xavier’s College under the University of Calcutta. He 
qualified with distinction as both a Chartered Accountant and 
a Cost and Management Accountant from the Indian institutes, 
securing a national silver medal in the CA examination in 1977. 
He is also a Fellow of CPA Australia (formerly CMA Australia 
and New Zealand).

Operational Excellence and Projects
Md. Saiful Alam oversees Marico’s end-to-end manufacturing 
operations, maintaining consistent delivery with maximum 
efficiency at budgeted cost. His leadership has resulted in 
reorganizing Marico manufacturing operations to yield the 
maximum benefits. He has led multiple projects in Marico 
Bangladesh Ltd, which includes Marico’s first Greenfield 
project in 2011, and several brownfield projects over the period. 
He was involved from business case preparation to execution 
of Marico’s biggest greenfield projects at National Special 
Economic Zone, Mirsarai. This factory started commercial 
production in July 2023. He ensures Marico’s manufacturing 
processes are efficient, sustainable, and aligned with the 
industry’s best practices. 
Strategic Vision and Institutional Leadership
Saiful is a member of Marico’s Leadership Team and contributed 
to business strategic decisions, especially on capability 
building in manufacturing and new product development. His 
26 Years of total experience Including international exposure 
with Nestle has enriched his leadership prowess, bringing a 
global perspective to Marico's manufacturing operations. 
Saiful's strategic vision drives continuous improvement and 
innovation in Marico's manufacturing processes. Saiful leads 
Marico’s Global ESG Goal in Bangladesh. He is a member of 
FICCI ESG Sub Committee and Bangladesh Sustainability 
Alliances (BSA) Working Group. He is also a member of 
Marico’s Inclusion & Diversity Council, to drive I&D agenda 
across the organization.
Academic Background and Interests
Saiful holds a BSc in Chemical Engineering Degree from BUET. 
He enjoys traveling, exploring different cultures and reading 
books, reflecting his holistic approach to life and leadership. 
These interests provide him with a well-rounded perspective 
that enhances his ability to lead a wide team and bring 
innovation in the processes.

RASHED BIN EHSHAN
Director - Legal & Corporate Affairs
Legal Expertise
Rashed Bin Ehshan has served as the Director of Legal and 
Corporate Affairs at Marico Bangladesh since June 2022. In 
this role, he oversees Corporate Social Responsibility (CSR), 
external communications, regulatory affairs, compliance and 
public relations. With over 19 years of experience in corporate 
governance, compliance management, dispute resolution, and 
advisory capacities, Rashed ensures that Marico operates in 
full compliance with legislative frameworks while upholding the 
highest ethical standards. His leadership drives the company's 
commitment to business sustainability and responsible 
corporate practices.
Professional Engagement
Before joining Marico, Rashed held senior positions at globally 
renowned companies such as Grameenphone, Coca-Cola, 
Maersk, and Nagad. His diverse and extensive experience has 
equipped him with a deep understanding of corporate and 
legal landscapes on an international scale. Rashed is actively 
involved in several prominent professional organizations, 
including the Foreign Investors' Chamber of Commerce and 
Industry (FICCI), the Metropolitan Chamber of Commerce and 
Industry (MCCI), and the Bangladesh Employers' Federation 
(BEF). His contributions to these bodies are instrumental in 
driving policy reforms, advocating for industry best practices, 
and shaping regulatory frameworks. Through his leadership, 
Rashed plays a pivotal role in advancing industry standards and 
fostering a collaborative environment for policy development.
Academic Credentials and Personal Interest
Rashed holds both a bachelor's and a master's degree in law 
and has been a member of the bar since 2008. Outside of his 
professional life, he is an avid reader, a music enthusiast and a 
culinary aficionado with a deep passion for travel and cultural 
exploration.

Awards : Parachute Advansed
Parachute Advansed Becomes the 2nd Most Chosen Personal Care Brand in Bangladesh 
Parachute Advansed began its journey in Bangladesh in 1999. Over the years, the brand has grown into one 
of the strongest players in the Personal Care landscape and has consistently led the Hair Oil category. Since 
its inception, the brand has consistently strengthened in equity metrices, expanded its distribution footprint, 
penetrated newer households and gained in market share across geographies. As a result, this year again, the 
brand has secured the 2nd position in the Personal Care for a number of years in a row now, as per Brand 
Footprint Report 2025 by Kantar Bangladesh. 
The ranking is a testament to the brand’s unwavering focus on quality and trust, reinforcing its position as a 
preferred choice for Bangladeshi families.

Dear Valued Shareholders,
It is with a profound sense of pride and a purposeful 
optimism grounded in evidence  that I present to you the 
performance of Marico Bangladesh Limited (“Company” 
or “MBL”) for the Financial Year ended 31 March 2026 (FY 
2025-26).
We live and operate in an era defined by complexity. 
Bangladesh's GDP growth moderated to 3.5% in FY 2024–
2025 (ended 30 June 2025), and the near-term economic 
outlook remains tempered by persistent inflationary 
pressures at 8.6%, tightening liquidity within the banking 
sector, and elevated freight and energy costs exacerbated 
by ongoing geopolitical conflict in the Middle East. Against 
this backdrop, the resilience MBL has demonstrated is 
neither coincidental nor circumstantial. It is the deliberate 
consequence of long-term strategic clarity, disciplined 
operational execution, and, above all, the unwavering 
commitment of our people.
Our financial results for FY 2025-26 bear testament to 
the enduring strength of our business model and the 
effectiveness of our growth strategy. Revenue expanded 
by 27%, while profit after tax reached BDT 649.2 crore, 
reflecting a growth of 9.9% - a top-percentile outcome in an 
environment that tested businesses across every sector. 
Our contribution to the national exchequer stood at BDT 
761 crore, underscoring not merely our commercial scale, 
but our deeply held sense of obligation to the nation whose 
trust and infrastructure have enabled our success.
In recognition of the Company's robust financial health 
and in fulfilment of our commitment to rewarding 
shareholder confidence, the Board has recommended a 
final dividend of 500% on face value, bringing the full-year 
payout to 2075%. This decision reflects our conviction in 
the Company's continued trajectory and our gratitude for 
the enduring trust you place in us.
At the heart of our performance lies the lasting power 
of our brands. Our flagship brand delivered a double-
digit growth during the year, reaffirming its position as 
a household name deeply embedded in the lives and 
daily rituals of Bangladeshi consumers. For the 16th 
consecutive year, Parachute Advansed has been honored 
as the No. 1 Most Loved Hair Oil Brand - a distinction that 
speaks not to momentary popularity, but to a bond of trust 
built across generations. We are equally proud that it has 
also been recognized as the 2nd Most Chosen Brand in the 
Personal Care category, a reflection of our relentless focus 
on quality, relevance, and meaningful consumer value.
Beyond our core franchise, the year was marked by 
exceptional momentum in other categories. Our Health 
and Beauty portfolio delivered a growth of 89%, validating 
Saugata Gupta
Chairman
the strategic investments we have made in product 
innovation, consumer insight and market development. 
Our expansion into Personal Care and Baby Care categories 
further reinforces our ambition to build a diversified, multi-
category FMCG powerhouse - one that accompanies 
the consumer across the full arc of life's most intimate 
moments.
We believe, with conviction, that long-term value creation 
must be pursued in a manner that is environmentally 
responsible and socially inclusive. Sustainability is not a 
peripheral commitment at MBL - it is central to our identity 
and integral to our strategy. During FY 2025-26, our 
sustainability initiatives generated approximately 250 MWh 
of renewable electricity through rooftop solar installations, 
reduced plastic generation by 12%, and delivered 
measurable reductions in greenhouse gas emissions 
through a range of energy-efficiency programs. Through 
SWAPNO - our flagship social impact program developed 
in partnership with the UNDP and the Government of 
Bangladesh - we continue to create pathways to financial 
inclusion and economic empowerment for ultra-poor rural 
women across the country. 
As we stand at the threshold of a new chapter, we do so with 
confidence tempered by humility, and optimism anchored 
in 
capability. 
Bangladesh's 
evolving 
demographics, 
rising consumer aspirations, and expanding middle 
class continue to create significant and largely untapped 
opportunities for growth. We will continue to invest in 
innovation, strengthen and extend our brand portfolio, 
enhance our digital and data capabilities, cultivate future-
ready leadership, and build the organizational agility 
needed to serve consumers whose expectations are 
constantly evolving.
On behalf of the Board, I extend my sincere appreciation 
to our members, management team, business partners, 
customers, government and regulatory partners for 
their continued support. Most of all, I thank you - our 
shareholders - for the enduring trust and confidence you 
place in MBL, a responsibility we do not take lightly.
Together, we will continue to build a stronger, more 
resilient, and more sustainable MBL - one that creates 
lasting value for all its stakeholders, and a meaningful 
legacy for Bangladesh.

Dear Valued Shareholders,
FY 2025-26 was, in every sense, a year of consequence. 
Against a backdrop of government transition, persistent 
inflationary pressures, and supply chain volatility, the 
operating environment in Bangladesh demanded of us not 
merely competence, but conviction. I am proud to report 
that Marico Bangladesh responded with both, delivering 
another year of strong, profitable, and purposeful growth.
Our financial results speak of the underlying strength of 
the business we have built together. Revenue grew by 27%, 
reaching BDT 2,071.2 crores, while profit after tax grew by 
9.9%, delivering earnings per share of BDT 206.1. Operating 
profit rose by 12.9% - a result that reflects not only strong 
topline momentum but disciplined stewardship of costs, 
capital, and capability.
These are not numbers that arrived by chance. They are 
the outcome of deliberate strategic choices: sustained 
investment behind our brands, rigorous operational 
discipline, and an organisation that has learned to adapt 
with agility rather than react with anxiety.
Our AAA credit rating with a Stable Outlook is a further 
affirmation of the financial integrity and risk management 
culture that underpins everything we do. It is a mark of 
confidence we do not take for granted.
At the heart of our performance lies the immense strength 
of our brand portfolio - one that today reaches nearly 90% 
of households across Bangladesh. That extraordinary 
level of consumer trust is our most durable competitive 
advantage, and our most solemn responsibility.
Throughout the year, we deepened our premiumisation 
agenda while broadening our consumer relevance. Our 
Health and Beauty segment grew 89%, driven by robust 
performance across premium variants while our expanded 
VAHO portfolio grew 26%. The Baby Care segment grew 
8%, reflecting steady penetration gains in a category we 
are committed to developing with patience and precision.
We brought meaningful innovation to market: Fiera 
America Pink Brightening Cream strengthened our Skin 
and Beauty footprint with a differentiated brightening 
proposition; Parachute Naturale Long Lasting Shine 
Shampoo, Parachute Advansed Extra Care with Vitamin 
E, and Parachute Advansed Total Care reinforced our 
haircare leadership. Each of these launches reflects a 
consumer-backed approach to innovation - one that 
begins with genuine insight and ends with genuine value.
Alongside portfolio expansion, we accelerated our sales 
transformation agenda - strengthening route-to-market 
capabilities, expanding our distribution footprint, and 
deepening our omni-channel presence to meet consumers 
wherever they choose to engage with us.
Strong topline performance was complemented by 
meaningful operational progress. Raw material cost 
pressures presented a real challenge - cost of sales 
grew at 59.3%, and our gross margin stood at 49.4%. 
We responded by driving efficiencies with rigour: a 16% 
reduction in specific power consumption at one of our key 
facilities, a 13% reduction in specific fuel consumption in 
crushing operations, and perhaps the achievement I am 
most personally proud of - a 55% reduction in market 
complaints, a testament to the quality culture our teams 
have built.
Across our manufacturing network, FY 2025-26 was 
marked by significant capacity expansion and operational 
strengthening. We enhanced export capabilities at our 
Shirirchala facility, expanded honey production and 
shampoo sachet-filling capacity at Mouchak plant, and 
increased product-filling capacity at our Mirsarai plant. 
Rooftop solar installations are already contributing 
meaningfully to our energy profile - a tangible signal of our 
commitment to decarbonising our operations over time.
For Marico Bangladesh, sustainability is not a program 
that runs alongside our business - it is woven into the 
very fabric of how we operate. We are advancing resource 
efficiency, responsible sourcing, and climate resilience 
across our value chain, with clear ambitions to reduce our 
environmental footprint as we grow.
Our social commitments are equally purposeful. Through 
our continued partnership with the United Nations 
Development Programme (UNDP) under the SWAPNO 
project, we are supporting the economic empowerment 
of some of Bangladesh's most vulnerable women and 
communities. During the year, the Company invested BDT 
10 million in corporate social responsibility initiatives, 
reaffirming our belief that business progress and human 
progress are, and must remain, inseparable.
None of these achievements would be possible without 
the talent, dedication, and spirit of the Marico family - on 
the factory floor, in the market, and across every office 
and function. In a year that tested everyone, our members 
chose to rise.
We continued to invest in building a future-ready 
organisation - attracting strong talent, deepening 
capabilities, 
and 
fostering 
an 
environment 
where 
individuals can grow, contribute, and belong. Our Diversity 
and Inclusion Council continued to champion a workplace 
culture rooted in equity and respect, reinforced by targeted 
initiatives around business integrity, workplace safety,

innovate, and the reason we hold ourselves to the highest 
standards. We do not take it lightly, and we never will.
The year behind us demonstrated what this organisation 
is capable of. The year ahead is an opportunity to surpass 
our previous landmarks.

Dear Valued Shareholders,
Looking back on FY 2025-26, what stands out is not the 
difficulty of the environment - though it was considerable - 
but how clearly our financial discipline held under pressure.
The macroeconomic environment was unsparing - elevated 
commodity prices, persistent inflationary pressures, and 
foreign exchange volatility created headwinds that tested 
businesses across the sector. Marico Bangladesh was 
not immune. But our response - measured, deliberate, 
and grounded in long-term thinking - is what I am most 
satisfied to report.
Revenue grew 27% to BDT 2,071.2 crores, reflecting broad-
based momentum across our portfolio. Operating profit 
reached BDT 800.8 crores, growing 12.9%, while profit after 
tax rose 9.9% to BDT 649.2 crores - delivering earnings per 
share of BDT 206.1, up from BDT 187.5 in the prior year.
I would like to present a candid view of the margin 
performance. Cost of sales increased by 59.3%, 
significantly outpacing revenue growth, which led to a 
compression in gross margin to 49.4% from 59.7%, and in 
net profit margin to 31.3% from 36.2%. This decline was 
primarily driven by elevated input costs and prevailing 
macroeconomic challenges during the year.
Our response to margin pressure was operational, 
not reactive. We optimised procurement, improved 
manufacturing efficiencies, and sharpened resource 
utilisation across the value chain. Marketing and 
distribution expenses optimised by 9.3% despite significant 
revenue growth - a result of genuine efficiency gains, not 
underinvestment. 
At our manufacturing facilities, a 16% reduction in specific 
power consumption and a 13% reduction in specific fuel 
usage in crushing operations reflect the kind of quiet, 
compounding progress that rarely makes headlines but 
consistently creates value.
Marico Bangladesh enters FY 2026-27 from a position 
of financial strength. Our equity base is robust, our 
AAA long-term credit rating with Stable Outlook 
was maintained, and we remained in full regulatory 
compliance throughout the year.
On capital allocation, we remain committed to balancing 
growth investment with meaningful shareholder returns. 
Total dividends for the year amount to 2075% on face value 
- comprising three interim dividends and a recommended 
final dividend of 500% - reflecting the Board's confidence 
in our cash generation and long-term prospects. The 
Company also contributed BDT 761 crores to the national 
exchequer, a 12% increase year-on-year, underscoring our 
role as a responsible corporate citizen.
We increasingly view sustainability not as a compliance 
function, but as a source of structural competitive advantage. 
Rooftop solar installations generated approximately 250 
MWh of renewable electricity during the year, reducing both 
our carbon footprint and our energy costs. 
I will not understate the near-term environment. Margin 
restoration will require continued execution discipline, 
and we are entering FY 2026-27 with clear priorities: 
accelerating profitable growth, improving productivity, 
investing in capacity and innovation, and maintaining the 
capital rigour that has served our stakeholders well.
Bangladesh's long-term fundamentals remain compelling. 
We are building a business designed to outperform across 
cycles - not just in favourable conditions.
My sincere appreciation goes to our members, partners, 
regulators, and shareholders for the trust they continue to 
place on us.
Shafiq Musharrof
Chief Financial Officer

At Marico we approach value creation from three principal lenses: our impact, our spectrum of engagement with stakeholders 
and managing key matters.
Our Impact 
As a multi-stakeholder organization dedicated to fostering sustainable value, we uniquely impact all of our stakeholder 
groups.
VALUE CREATION 
All figures are for the Financial Year 2025-26
Fostering Meaningful Connections with Our Stakeholders 
We remain steadfast in strengthening our engagement with 
all stakeholder groups to foster sustainable value creation. 
At Marico, stakeholders are identified based on their roles, 
depth of involvement, contributions across the value chain, 
and their capacity to influence business outcomes. These 
encompass our consumers, shareholders, employees, 
business partners, regulators and government authorities, 
as well as the communities we serve.

Managing Key Material Matters 
Material matters are those that substantively affect our 
ability to create value over time
Stakeholders provide Marico with critical resources that 
enable the Company to generate competitive sustainable 
value over the short, medium and long term. Engaging with 
our stakeholders allows us to understand their viewpoints 
and expectations. The feedback thus generated helps us 
to develop effective strategies for sustainable long-term 
value creation through the refinement of our products and 
services and our go-to-market strategies. 
We review our material matters during our annual planning 
cycle, our strategic reviews and monthly work-plans. 
The management team makes an initial assessment – 
drawing on their experience, judgement, and their own 
engagement with stakeholders – and agree which matters 
may be material and the response measures to address 
the materiality.  
Identification of Key Material Matters
The issues identified through stakeholder engagement 
are analysed, prioritised and categorised based on their 
ability to affect Marico’s ability to create long-term value, 
summarized as follows:

Overview
Financial Year 2025-26 (FY 2026) have marked another year of Marico Bangladesh maintaining strong financial performance 
that demonstrates the resilience and strong foundation of the Company. This financial year was characterized by political 
uncertainty on the domestic front and conflict in the Middle East on the international front, posing challenges for businesses 
and consumers alike throughout FY 2026. Despite the challenges posed by external factors, the Company remained 
committed to delivering strong financial results. Our culture of combining innovation and efficiency has continued to deliver 
strong financial results and dividends to our shareholders. We also reinforced our commitment to improve our human 
capital and sustainability whilst creating a positive impact on the wider community we serve. Allocation of our financial 
capital was conducted with the objective of delivering long-term competitive advantage and maximizing shareholder value.

Performance Overview
The gross profit of the company grew by 5.2% during FY 2026, despite increase in prices of key raw materials. This was 
achieved through 27% revenue growth and strategic sourcing and inventory management. The net profit of the company 
grew by 9.9%, reflecting the successful execution of cost-management strategy.

Cost Management
In FY 2026, Marico Bangladesh maintained its discipline in overhead management along with strategic sourcing practices 
in order to maintain strong profit margin in the face of rising raw material prices. Our marketing strategy focused on 
prioritizing campaigns with high impact whilst maintaining high standard of product quality. These measures, supported 
by data-driven decision-making, helped to absorb external pressures and contributed to a healthy EBITDA margin of 37.7%, 
underpinning our financial resilience and enabling continued investment in growth and innovation.

OUR MANUFACTURING 
CAPITAL
Marico Bangladesh Limited is a top tier multinational FMCG company in Bangladesh 
and remains a key player in the beauty and wellness industry. Our broad and diversified 
portfolio includes well established brands such as Parachute Coconut Oil, Parachute 
Advanced, Parachute Naturale Shampoo, Just for Baby, and Saffola. These trusted brands 
are developed to meet the changing and evolving needs and preferences of our consumers 
across different product categories and usage occasions.
OVERVIEW
Marico Bangladesh runs three state of the art production plants, supported by four distribution centers and two storage 
facilities that strengthen our nationwide logistics system. To improve agility and ensure effective use of assets, we 
follow an asset light strategy by collaborating with two dependable third party manufacturing partners. This connected 
setup allows smooth manufacturing flow and market service, helping us always keep our products available across 
the country.
Environmental stewardship and social commitment remain central to how we conduct our business. We have adopted 
advanced process improvements to reduce ecological impact while maintaining strict compliance in our operations. 
Beyond factory boundaries, we actively participate in programs that promote local communities, protect natural resources, 
and support social progress. We continue to provide dependable and cost effective solutions that add value to consumers. 
By improving processes and following principled workplace standards, we address changing customer expectations while 
strengthening our position in the sector.
MANUFACTURING HIGHLIGHTS OF SHIRIRCHALA FACTORY
In line with our vision for sustainable business growth and rising acceptance 
of Marico products in international markets, we strengthened our export 
capability during financial year 2025-2026 (FY’26). Automation and design 
modification were adopted across filling lines to enhance process efficiency and 
throughput for export enhancement. This initiative resulted in 150% increase 
in export volume in FY’26 compared to financial year 2024-2025 (FY’25). The 
improvement reflects growing customer trust and demand.
Expanding 
Export 
Volume
During FY’26, honey production volume increased by 25% across different SKUs. 
This growth was driven by capacity enhancement through installation of new 
capping machine and modification of filling machine. This progress supports 
business expansion plans and reflects growing consumer demand for Marico 
Honey products in the market.
Growth 
in Honey 
Production 
Volume
In FY’26, the refinery cooling tower was redesigned and upgraded to reduce water 
consumption, increase cooling efficiency and improve process performance. This 
enhancement increased overall efficiency in refinery operations and supported 
higher production yield, contributing to better resource utilization and improved 
manufacturing effectiveness.
Cooling 
Tower 
Optimization


--- PDF page 89 ---

Leadership 
Messages
Value 
Creation
An in house project was undertaken to reuse ETP outlet water in cooling tower by 
executing required engineering modifications. This enabled sustainable reuse of 
treated water, reducing overall water consumption and supporting environmental 
objectives. Through this initiative, reuse of ETP treated water can potentially save 
up to 75 KL of groundwater annually, strengthening our sustainability commitment.
ETP Water 
Re-use in 
Cooling 
Tower
As part of our continued focus on delivering superior product quality, we introduced 
advanced technologies in Quality Assurance during FY’26. Key upgrades include 
installation of a latest model NIR machine for coconut oil quality assessment and a 
hydro press filter to ensure improved product purity. These initiatives strengthen our 
commitment to providing safe and high quality products to customers.
Advanced 
QA 
Technology 
Integration
Driving Process Efficiency and Resource Optimization 
Marico Bangladesh continued to focus on reducing material loss and improving process efficiency during FY’26. Filling line 
capacity was enhanced without increasing power or fuel cost, supported by the adoption of latest technologies to minimize 
process loss. Packaging material loss was significantly reduced along with near zero market complaints. Solar power initiatives 
were introduced as our commitment to sustainable initiatives. In addition, improvements in fuel usage and production 
processes lowered operational losses. These actions helped increase efficiency, reduce waste, and support sustainable 
manufacturing growth through continuous improvement.
Increase in Refinery Yield
Strategic process enhancements helped increase refinery yield by 1.3% in FY’26 compared to FY’25, which 
is equivalent to a saving of BDT 220 Lacs. This improvement highlights our strong commitment to maximize 
resource utilization through continuous process development and operational efficiency.
Crushing Extraction Efficiency
Through enhanced process control, equipment optimization, and operating parameter improvement in the 
coconut crushing unit, extraction efficiency increased by 0.4% from FY’25 to FY’26. This improvement resulted 
in a saving of BDT 134 Lacs, demonstrating our ongoing focus on operational excellence and effective resource 
utilization.

MANUFACTURING HIGHLIGHTS OF MOUCHAK FACTORY
Shampoo Batch Capacity Improvement
45% Shampoo batch-making capacity increased through process innovation. Key initiatives included batch-making 
optimization, vessel modification, transfer line modification and automation resulting in improved throughput 
efficiency and operational robustness.
Shampoo Sachet Filling Capacity Improvement
25% Shampoo Sachet filling capacity improved by upgrading existing machines. The initiative will deliver BDT 35 lac 
operational savings and reduced capital investment by 52% against a new machine.
Reduction in Specific Fuel Consumption
Fuel consumption in the crushing unit reduced 
by 13% as SFC decreased from 9.3 Unit/MT in 
FY’25 to 8.1 Unit/MT in FY’26 through process 
improvements and better operational control. This 
reduction helped minimize fuel cost and resulted 
in a cost saving of BDT 32 Lac, contributing to 
improved efficiency and lower overall energy 
usage in manufacturing operations.

commissioned to support growth in shampoo demand. 
The investment strengthened production capacity, 
ensured supply continuity, and enhanced operational 
readiness to meet accelerating market requirements.
High Speed Oil Filling Infrastructure at Mouchak
A high-speed Coconut Oil filling machine was 
successfully commissioned at the Mouchak facility 
as part of the business contingency plan. The initiative 
enhanced operational resilience, will ensure supply 
continuity and will strengthen preparedness against 
potential production disruptions.
Marico Bangladesh’s First Ever Vision System (OCR/Presence-Absence) Technology implementation
An advanced Vision System with OCR and Presence‑Absence functionality was installed to strengthen packaging quality and 
compliance. The solution ensures accurate print verification and eliminates dependency on manual inspection, significantly 
reducing the risk of human error. Faulty or incorrectly coded products are automatically identified and segregated, 
minimizing chances of defects. The system also generates traceable digital records for every verified print, supporting audit 
readiness, regulatory compliance, and robust quality assurance. Overall, this initiative has enhanced process reliability, 
improved customer confidence, and reinforced the organization’s commitment to quality excellence.

Capacity Enhancement and Efficiency
Water Based OEE Improvement
Water Based OEE has shown a strong and consistent 
improvement over the last five years, increasing 
from 70.3% in FY’22 to 81.5% in FY’26. The 2.5% 
improvement in FY’26 over FY’25 and the cumulative 
11.2% gain since FY’22 reflect the effectiveness of 
focused process improvements, equipment reliability 
initiatives, and disciplined execution of operational 
excellence programs. This sustained upward trend 
demonstrates the team’s continued commitment to 
productivity optimization and manufacturing efficiency.
PET Plant OEE Improvement
PET Plant OEE has demonstrated strong and sustained 
improvement, increasing from 77.8% in FY’22 to 87.7% 
in FY’26. The 1.1% improvement in FY’26 over FY’25 
and the cumulative 9.9% gain since FY’22 underline the 
success of focused maintenance strategies, process 
stability, and continuous improvement initiatives.
Water Based SPC Improvement
9% Water‑based Specific Power Consumption (SPC) 
was reduced in FY’26, declining from 160 KWH/KL 
in FY’25 to 145 KWH/KL. This improvement reflects 
enhanced energy discipline, process optimization 
initiatives, and sustained focus on improving operational 
energy efficiency across water‑based manufacturing 
operations.
Water Based SFC Improvement
30% Specific fuel consumption for the boiler was 
reduced, from 14.77 to 10.37 L/KL, through in‑house 
development of a smart heating coil replacing traditional 
jacket heating. The initiative halved water heating time, 
cut diesel consumption, reduced carbon footprint, and 
delivered significant cost savings with minimal CAPEX. 
36,635L less Boiler Diesel will be required in FY’27 
which is equivalent to 32.8 Lac BDT Savings.

2000KL Capacity Tank for LLP Storage (BCP)

QUALITY EXCELLENCE-KEY HIGHLIGHTS AND ACHIEVEMENTS
The Quality Excellence journey this year reflects significant progress across key performance areas, demonstrating 
our strong commitment to continuous improvement and customer satisfaction. In FY'26, we achieved 55.3% reduction 
in market complaints. The introduction of advanced analytical instruments strengthened our testing and control 
capabilities. Our robust execution of NPD/EPD initiatives delivered zero product recalls with 100% Right First-Time 
performance, while sustained compliance with global standards—QMS (ISO 9001:2015), Codex GMP, and Cosmetics 
GMP (ISO 22716:2007)—further reinforces our dedication to quality, safety, and regulatory excellence.
Introduction of New Analytical Instruments
To strengthen analytical capabilities, five advanced instruments were installed across our laboratories: NIR Machine, Abbe 
Refractometer, and Soxhlet Apparatus at Shirirchala; Lovibond Tintometer and Cone Penetrometer at Mouchak; and a Top 
Load Tester at Mirsarai. Among these, the NIR (Near Infrared) Machine stands out as the most sophisticated, significantly 
enhancing process monitoring, reducing analysis time and cost, and improving accuracy and repeatability. Compared to 
conventional wet chemistry, NIR enables rapid, non destructive analysis, supporting real time decision-making, better process 
control, and greater laboratory efficiency.

Stories of Strong Friendship: Friendship Day Campaign by Parachute Advansed
Parachute Advansed continues to lead Bangladesh’s hair 
care segment by consistently delivering on its promise of 
‘Strong Hair’—a proposition that strongly resonates with 
consumers. Through thoughtfully designed, contextual 
campaigns under the theme ‘Strong Roots Make You 
Beautiful,’ activated around key occasions such as 
Mother’s Day, Friendship Day, and Women’s Day, the 
brand reinforces its equity across diverse age cohorts.
Anchored in the ‘Strong Hair Strong Bond’ platform, 
Parachute Advansed links hair‑oiling rituals with the 
nurturing of lasting relationships. By celebrating these 
meaningful connections, the brand instils a sense of 
pride among consumers, further cementing its position 
as a trusted haircare staple.
To celebrate Friendship Day, Parachute Advansed launched 
an engaging campaign titled ‘Stories of Strong Friendship’, 
aimed at celebrating the essence of companionship. The 
initiative invited consumers to share nostalgic memories 
and meaningful moments with their close circles of friends, 
highlighting the enduring nature of lifelong bonds. 
By enabling collective storytelling, the brand deepened 
its connection with a wider audience, driving strong 
social media engagement and reinforcing its positioning 
as a brand that celebrates the ‘strong bonds’ central to 
consumers’ lives.
Beautiful Memories with My Mom: Mother’s Day 
Campaign by Parachute Advansed 
As part of its Mother’s Day celebration, Parachute 
Advansed rolled out ‘Beautiful Memories with My Mom’, 
an emotional engagement‑led campaign. Consumers 
were encouraged to submit old, faded photographs with 
their mothers, which were restored using technology. 
The campaign successfully revived nostalgic moments, 
driving high engagement by tapping into deep emotional 
and personal connections.

Project Prottoyi: Translating Resilience into Real World Impact
To bring its resilience pillar to life, Parachute Advansed launched 
Project Prottoyi—a strategic initiative aimed at addressing the 
psychological and social barriers faced by adolescent girls in 
underprivileged communities. These girls often carry a 'double 
burden', navigating the emotional transitions of adolescence 
alongside systemic constraints such as restricted mobility and 
limited access to mental‑health support.
Delivered through an intensive five‑day program, the initiative reached 
680 students across six schools, equipping them with a practical 
toolkit for resilience. By focusing on confidence‑building, cognitive 
mindset, and emotional self‑care, Project Prottoyi moved beyond 
conventional learning to empower young girls with the skills needed 
to overcome structural challenges and unlock their future potential.
For Strong and Beautiful Hair, Use Parachute Advansed 3 Days a Week
To reinforce healthy hair‑care habits, Parachute Advansed launched 
the “3 Days a Week” campaign - a strategic habit‑change initiative. 
The campaign leveraged two distinct films, each tailored to the 
unique needs, life stages, and lifestyles of different demographic 
segments, encouraging consumers to consciously integrate hair 
oiling into their routines three times a week.
This cohort‑specific communication approach enhanced message 
relevance and relatability, while repositioning frequent hair oiling as a 
critical pillar of effective hair care rather than an occasional practice. 
Stories of Resilience: Women’s Day Campaign by Parachute 
Advansed
This Women’s Day, Parachute Advansed made a purposeful effort to 
celebrate women who exemplify perseverance in their everyday lives 
and whose journeys of overcoming adversity inspire others.
Through the campaign ‘Resilience to Become the Person You Are’, 
consumers were invited to share personal stories of challenges 
they had overcome and the experiences that shaped who they are 
today. By spotlighting these powerful narratives of inner strength, 
the brand built a deep emotional connection with its audience, 
driving meaningful conversations and engagement while reinforcing 
Parachute Advansed’s role as a champion of resilience.

Parachute Advansed Total Care: A New Launch to Address ‘Long and 
Strong’ Hair Care Need
Say Yes to Silky and Beautiful Hair, Say Yes to Parachute Naturale Shampoo
Get long Lasting Shiny hair with Parachute Naturale Long Lasting Shine Shampoo
Parachute Advansed Total Care was introduced to tap into long and 
strong haircare needs—a strategic opportunity within the Hair Care 
category. The intervention was specifically designed to meet unmet 
consumer need for a hair oil that delivers the combined benefits of ‘Long 
and Strong’ hair—two of the most sought‑after functional outcomes in 
the category.
As a new member in the trusted house of Parachute Advansed, 
Parachute Advansed Total Care offers a holistic solution aligned to 
these core hair‑care aspirations. 
Parachute Naturale Shampoo was launched in Q1 of FY 20–21 with 
three variants - Nourishing Care, Anti Hair Fall, and Damage Repair. 
Since its introduction, the brand has steadily built strong consumer 
trust, anchored in its core promise, “Say Yes to Parachute Naturale 
Shampoo,” which emphasizes the use of safe, natural ingredients. 
All variants are paraben‑free and dermatologically tested, reinforcing 
the brand’s commitment to gentle yet effective hair care. On the 
strength of this proposition and growing consumer credibility, 
Parachute Naturale has emerged as the second most penetrated 
shampoo brand in the country.
Coconut Milk Protein is the hero ingredient in each variant, while 
other potent ingredients such as Aloe Vera, Black Olive, Onion, 
Hibiscus, Henna and Egg are added to deliver a range of haircare 
benefits to the beauty conscious women of the country. 
Launched in September 2025, Parachute Naturale 
reinforced its shampoo portfolio with the introduction 
of Parachute Naturale Long‑Lasting Shine Shampoo, 
crafted for consumers seeking naturally radiant, 
long‑lasting shine. Enriched with Coconut Milk Protein 
and a Black Olive Shine‑Boosting Formula, the variant 
delivers 2× shine that lasts up to 48 hours.
Coconut Milk Protein deeply nourishes hair from 
the roots, and strengthens it, while Black Olive’s 
shine‑boosting properties restore natural moisture, 
leaving hair silky and visibly shinier. Together, the 
formulation offers an effective balance of nourishment 
and shine enhancement.
Since launch, the variant has received encouraging 
consumer response, enhancing the brand’s relevance 
among shine‑seeking cohorts.

Parachute Just for Baby: So Safe Recommended by Mothers
Parachute Just for Baby is Marico Bangladesh’s baby care 
range, built on the promise of “100% Safe Ingredients” and 
Natural Nourishment for baby’s delicate skin. Designed as 
an international-standard solution for parents, the brand is 
positioned around gentle care that is recommended by lacs of 
mothers in the country. The range is Australian Allergy Certified 
and free from harmful chemicals like paraben and sulphates, 
ensuring high safety standards. The portfolio offers a complete 
care routine including nourishing baby oils, mild lotions, no-tears 
shampoos, and moisturising face creams, each formulated to 
protect and gently care for naturally soft baby skin. Coming 
from the trusted house of Parachute Advansed, Parachute Just 
for Baby continues to be the safe baby care solution for lacs of 
mothers in the country. 
Fiera Brightening Cream: Introducing Pink Glow 
Beauty in Bangladesh
Soft and Moisturized Skin from Parachute SkinPure 
Body Lotion
Fiera Brightening Cream is Marico Bangladesh’s 
latest innovation, launched in August 2025. It offers 
a unique “Pink Glow” powered by American Deep 
Bright Technology and a mix of Rose Serum and Milk 
Vitamins. It reduces dark spots and delivers up to 12 
hours of pink brightness.
The Parachute SkinPure portfolio brings together 
the goodness of natural ingredients and the trust of 
Parachute to deliver soft, glowing, and moisturized skin. 
The range comes in three variants: Natural Moisture, 
Natural White and Deep Moisture, each built around 
natural ingredients such as Coconut Milk, Olive, Shea 
Butter and Sandalwood to suit different skin needs. 
The promise is simple: softer, smoother skin drawn 
from nature itself. Every drop works to nourish deeply, 
leaving skin naturally radiant, and providing the kind of 
softness you can see and feel.

Livon promises Salon Ready Hair in just One Minute
Stay younger looking Naturally with Hair Code Egypt
Livon is a trendsetter in the transformational beauty space, 
backed by decades of haircare expertise and trusted by 
consumers worldwide. Renowned for delivering instant, 
visible results, the brand understands the modern consumer’s 
need for quick, effective solutions that seamlessly integrate 
into everyday routines.
Livon Silk‑n‑Shine Hair Serum, enriched with the goodness of 
Aloe Vera and Vitamin E, is crafted to transform hair in just one 
minute. It instantly makes hair twice as silky, five times shinier, 
and provides double the frizz control—leaving hair looking 
healthy, smooth, and effortlessly manageable. Its lightweight, 
non‑greasy formula helps nourish and protect hair from dryness 
and environmental stress while enhancing natural shine.
With Livon, achieving salon‑ready hair is no longer reserved 
for special occasions—it’s an everyday reality.
In 2022, HairCode introduced HairCode Egypt 
Mehedi Crème Color—a category‑first innovation that 
transformed traditional Mehedi into a convenient, 
ready‑to‑use crème format for both hair and beard. 
The product eliminates the need for grinding or mixing 
leaves, delivering natural Mehedi colour in just 30 
minutes. Enriched with Natural Mehedi and hibiscus, 
and free from harmful chemicals, it allows consumers 
to achieve three intensity levels based on application 
time—15, 30, or 60 minutes. The launch was supported 
by communication that strongly emphasized ease of 
use and convenience.
In the same year, HairCode further expanded its 
innovation pipeline with the launch of HairCode Egypt 
Shampoo Hair Color, a five‑minute application format 
infused with Amla, Methi, and Mehedi to deliver a 
natural black shade. Together, these innovations 
address evolving consumer expectations for faster, 
safer, and hassle‑free hair colouring solutions.

SALES HIGHLIGHTS
DISTRIBUTION AND GO TO MARKET 
EXCELLENCE
In financial year 2025-26 (FY’26), Marico Bangladesh 
sustained its sharp focus on strengthening Go To Market 
(GTM) excellence, deepening outlet level service quality, 
and enhancing in store availability. Guided by the unifying 
philosophy of delivering the “Right service in the right 
outlets at the right time,” the Company further optimized 
its frontline architecture to unlock incremental growth, 
execution precision, and long term competitiveness.
Go To Market Initiatives
To 
intensify 
service 
delivery 
and 
improve 
beat 
effectiveness, Marico strengthened its frontline ecosystem 
through the induction of additional 4% Distribution Sales 
Representatives (DSRs), alongside a strategic increase in 
market sections. These structural enhancements enabled 
sharper beat focus, higher service frequency, and stronger 
execution consistency across both numeric and weighted 
distribution.
FY’26 also marked the rollout of Must Sale SKU discipline 
across the GTM ecosystem, ensuring the consistent 
availability of strategically critical SKUs at the outlet 
level. This initiative significantly improved outlet wise 
assortment quality and unique placements, resulting 
in a 15% increase in product line availability per outlet. 
The strengthened assortment not only enhanced brand 
visibility but also translated into superior off take through 
improved portfolio representation at the point of sale.
Transformation through IT Infrastructure and Data 
Driven Business Analytics
FY’26 was a pivotal year in advancing Marico’s digital first 
sales ecosystem, with heightened emphasis on real time 
visibility, transparency, and frontline empowerment. Sales 
IT capabilities were further reinforced to ensure faster data 
led decision making and agile performance management.
All DSRs were equipped with real time program enrollment 
visibility, till date performance dashboards, and KPI 
improvement trackers—providing a clear line of sight on 
targets, achievements, and corrective actions. These 
interventions strengthened ownership at the frontline, 
enhanced daily execution discipline, and enabled proactive 
course correction at both field and managerial levels.
Customer experience was further elevated through the 
rollout of end to end trade return (L&D) automation, 
including visibility of L&D values on retailer invoices. This 
initiative enhanced transparency with trade partners, 
reinforced Marico’s commitment to customer satisfaction, 
and positioned L&D as a tangible value proposition—
deepening trust and long term partnerships.

Recognizing that sustainable business performance 
is fueled by a capable and motivated frontline, Marico 
intensified its focus on sales capability development in

A major milestone was achieved with 100% DSR training

6,000

NEXT GENERATION MARICO (NGM) 
VERTICAL
Building on the successful introduction of the Next 
Generation Marico (NGM) distribution model in FY’24, 
FY’26 marked a year of execution, validation, and 
disciplined scale up. The prototype, launched earlier, was 
refined through on ground learnings, system integration, 
and 
capability 
enhancement—enabling 
structured 
expansion across priority markets.
During the year, NGM significantly strengthened Marico’s 
presence in competitive and emerging personal care 
categories, accelerated premiumization, and reinforced 
a sharp focus on future ready portfolios. By the close 
of FY’26, NGM had evolved from a pilot initiative into 
a strategic growth engine, well positioned for broader 
national expansion and sustained long term value creation.
FY’26 also represented the first full year of operationalizing 
NGM beyond its pilot phase. The Company focused 
on embedding GTM principles, strengthening channel 
fundamentals, and sharpening portfolio prioritization 
across high potential segments. Learnings from early 
rollout enabled refinement of execution frameworks, 
expansion of reach, and enhanced organizational 
readiness—cementing NGM’s role as a future facing 
distribution vertical driving innovation and growth.

SUPPLY CHAIN HIGHLIGHTS
Strengthening Supply Chain Capabilities for Sustainable Growth
In an increasingly dynamic business environment, a resilient and agile supply chain remains central to sustaining growth 
and delivering value. Over the year, we strengthened our end-to-end supply chain capabilities through capacity expansion, 
sharper procurement strategies, enhanced customer responsiveness, and continued investments in people, systems, and 
operational excellence. These efforts have improved service delivery, increased efficiency, and reinforced our readiness to 
support the evolving needs of the business.
To support growing business requirements and 
strengthen supply assurance, we undertook 
manufacturing capacity augmentation initiatives at 
our Mouchak and MEZ plants. These enhancements 
have improved operational flexibility, increased 
throughput, and created stronger readiness to meet 
rising market demand. By investing in capacity ahead 
of need, we are building a more robust manufacturing 
backbone for future growth.
We made significant progress in warehouse 
optimization through consolidation initiatives aimed 
at improving space utilization, reducing complexity, 
and enhancing operational control. This has enabled 
better inventory visibility, more efficient storage and 
handling, and improved distribution effectiveness. 
The resulting network efficiency supports faster 
decision-making and more streamlined execution 
across the supply chain.
Continuous improvement remained a key focus 
area during the year, with MARVAL continuing 
to guide our efforts in unlocking efficiency and 
value across the supply chain. Through disciplined 
problem-solving, process simplification, and targeted 
cost optimization initiatives, we further improved 
operational effectiveness while maintaining quality 
and compliance standards. MARVAL continues to 
play an important role in embedding a culture of 
value-conscious execution across teams.
In a volatile sourcing environment, we continued to 
sharpen our procurement approach to maximize 
buying efficiency and strengthen value delivery. 
Through better planning, stronger supplier 
engagement, and disciplined negotiation, we 
improved procurement outcomes while ensuring 
continuity of supply. Our strategic procurement 
focus has helped us navigate market volatility more 
effectively and contribute meaningfully to overall 
business performance.
